Seeds = region faces jet-interior-first (ascending \`neighbor_jet_mismatch\`); per seed grow each jet-pruned candidate (\`grow::<S>\`) and commit the LARGEST validated area (DOF tiebreak); claim resolution assigns multi-claimed junction faces to the best claimant (2× margin); leftovers → per-facet. \`grow\` = seed_patch (jet-similar, DEPTH-LIMITED BFS \`MAX_PATCH_DEPTH=3\`, cap 32, magnitude within 1.5×) → fit → \`flood\` (connected region faces validating the fit) → refit fixpoint. THREE hard-won fixes: (1) **flood tolerance = 30× vertex-tolerance** (\`GROW_VTOL_FACTOR\`) — a fit from partial support has ~1e-4mm residual and a coarse "flat" reads ~1e-3mm plane residual, both >> the 5e-5mm vtol yet << the ~0.1mm a neighbor diverges one row past a tangent junction; committed hyps are refit on full support (~1e-6mm) and claim resolution fixes one-row leaks. 1	//! Stage 2.3(b): scoped fit competition.
2	//!
3	//! For a smooth region the whole-region ladder (2.3(a)) cannot explain — a
4	//! tangent chain like the rounded-cube shell (6 planes, 12 fillet cylinders,
5	//! 8 corner spheres) or the nosecone's cone+sphere — decompose it by
6	//! seed-first multi-type competition run INSIDE the region. The sharp
7	//! boundary cordons the arena, so competition cost is bounded and
8	//! cross-region accidents are impossible.
9	//!
10	//! - **Seeds**: region faces not yet claimed by any committed hypothesis, in
11	//!   jet-interior-first order (faces whose (κ1,κ2) best match their
12	//!   neighbors' seed before junction rows). Claimed faces never seed.
13	//! - **Per seed**: grow each candidate surface type (jet-pruned) from a
14	//!   small jet-similar seed patch — fit, flood-accept every region face the
15	//!   fit validates, refit on the grown support, to a fixpoint — and commit
16	//!   the hypothesis with the **largest validated area** (simplest-DOF
17	//!   tiebreak). The jet only orders and prunes; vertex-tolerance validation
18	//!   decides every face.
19	//! - **Claims**: a flood may grow through faces already claimed by an
20	//!   earlier hypothesis (junction rows that genuinely lie on two surfaces),
21	//!   so a face can be claimed by several hypotheses. \`resolve_claims\`
22	//!   assigns each contested face to the claimant that fits its vertices best
23	//!   (2× margin to leave the larger claimant).
24	//!
25	//! **Soundness** (the sub-region lemma, under exactness): an accidental fit
26	//! is supported on a *sub-region*; the true surface fitted from an interior
27	//! seed floods — vertex tolerance the arbiter — to its entire true extent,
28	//! and largest-validated-area then selects it over the accidental one.
29	//!
30	//! Faces no hypothesis claims are returned as leftovers; the caller degrades
31	//! them to per-facet planes (freeform fitting is stage 2.3(e), a later step).
32	
33	use std::collections::HashSet;
34	
35	use crate::config::Config;
36	use crate::stage1::ConnectedMesh;

38	use super::bfs::SurfaceType;

42	use super::regions::Region;
43	use super::spherical::SphericalSurface;
44	use super::{
45	    face_area, ConicalHypothesis, PlanarHypothesis, SphericalHypothesis, MIN_CYLINDER_FACES,
46	    MIN_SPHERE_FACES,
47	};
48	
49	/// Max faces in the jet-similar seed patch used to condition the initial fit.
50	const SEED_PATCH_CAP: usize = 32;
51	
52	/// Max BFS depth of the seed patch. Kept shallow so the patch stays local to
53	/// the seed and does not bleed across a tangent junction between two surfaces
54	/// of the same curvature class (e.g. a cone meeting a cylinder, where the
55	/// curvature is continuous across the seam) — a straddling patch fits neither
56	/// surface. An interior seed's shallow neighbourhood is on one surface, which
57	/// is all the initial fit needs; the flood then grows it to the true extent.
58	const MAX_PATCH_DEPTH: usize = 3;
59	
60	/// Refit-and-reflood rounds while growing one hypothesis.
61	const MAX_GROW_ROUNDS: usize = 8;
62	
63	/// Flood-acceptance tolerance as a multiple of \`--vertex-tolerance\`. A fit
64	/// grown from partial support has ~1e-4 mm residual and a coarsely-tessellated
65	/// "flat" face reads a small nonzero curvature (plane residual ~1e-3 mm), both
66	/// well above the 5e-5 mm default vertex tolerance yet far below the ~0.1 mm a
67	/// neighbouring surface diverges by one row past a tangent junction — so a
68	/// modest loosening bootstraps the grow without leaking across junctions.
69	/// Any residual one-row leak is corrected by claim resolution, and every
70	/// committed hypothesis is refit on its full support (accurate to ~1e-6 mm).
71	const GROW_VTOL_FACTOR: f64 = 30.0;
72	
73	/// A committed analytic hypothesis from competition, tagged by type.
74	pub(super) enum CompeteHyp {
75	    Planar(PlanarHypothesis),
76	    Conical(ConicalHypothesis),
77	    Spherical(SphericalHypothesis),
78	}
79	
80	impl CompeteHyp {
81	    fn faces(&self) -> &[usize] {
82	        match self {
83	            CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => &h.faces,
84	            CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => &h.faces,
85	            CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => &h.faces,
86	        }
87	    }
88	    fn set_faces(&mut self, faces: Vec<usize>, verts: Vec<usize>) {
89	        match self {
90	            CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => {
91	                h.faces = faces;
92	                h.vertices = verts;
93	            }
94	            CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => {
95	                h.faces = faces;
96	                h.vertices = verts;
97	            }
98	            CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => {
99	                h.faces = faces;
100	                h.vertices = verts;
101	            }
102	        }
103	    }
104	}
105	
106	/// Decompose a tangent-chain region. Returns the committed analytic
107	/// hypotheses (a strict partition after claim resolution) plus the leftover
108	/// faces no hypothesis explained.
109	pub(super) fn compete_region(
110	    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
111	    region: &Region,
112	    jet: &[JetField],
113	    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
114	    config: &Config,
115	) -> (Vec<CompeteHyp>, Vec<usize>) {
116	    let n = mesh.faces.len();
117	    let debug = config.verbosity >= 3 || std::env::var("BREPPER_DEBUG_COMPETE").is_ok();
118	    let mut in_region = vec![false; n];
119	    for &fi in &region.faces {
120	        in_region[fi] = true;
121	    }
122	
123	    // Region curvature scale: a face reads "flat" when its principal
124	    // curvature radius exceeds a couple of region diameters.
125	    let extent = region_extent(mesh, region);
126	    let flat_eps = if extent > 0.0 { 1.0 / (2.0 * extent) } else { 1e-6 };
127	
128	    // Seed order: jet-interior-first (ascending curvature mismatch with
129	    // region neighbors), ties by face index for determinism.
130	    let mut seeds = region.faces.clone();
131	    let score: Vec<f64> = (0..n)
132	        .map(|fi| {
133	            if !in_region[fi] {
134	                return f64::INFINITY;
135	            }
136	            neighbor_jet_mismatch(mesh, fi, jet, &in_region)
137	        })
138	        .collect();
139	    seeds.sort_by(|&a, &b| {
140	        score[a]
141	            .partial_cmp(&score[b])
142	            .unwrap()
143	            .then_with(|| a.cmp(&b))
144	    });
145	
146	    let mut claimed = vec![false; n]; // out of the seed pool once claimed
147	    let mut committed: Vec<CompeteHyp> = Vec::new();
148	    let mut claim_lists: Vec<Vec<usize>> = vec![Vec::new(); n]; // face -> committed idxs
149	
150	    for &seed in &seeds {
151	        if claimed[seed] {
152	            continue;
153	        }
154	        let jf = jet[seed];
155	        let a1 = jf.k1.abs();
156	        let a2 = jf.k2.abs();
157	
158	        // Grow the jet-pruned candidate types; keep the largest validated
159	        // area (simplest-DOF tiebreak).
160	        let mut best: Option<(CompeteHyp, f64, u8)> = None; // (hyp, area, dof)
161	        let consider = |hyp_area: Option<(CompeteHyp, f64)>, dof: u8, best: &mut Option<(CompeteHyp, f64, u8)>| {
162	            if let Some((h, area)) = hyp_area {
163	                let better = match best {
164	                    None => true,
165	                    Some((_, ba, bd)) => area > *ba + 1e-9 || ((area - *ba).abs() <= 1e-9 && dof < *bd),
166	                };
167	                if better {
168	                    *best = Some((h, area, dof));
169	                }
170	            }
171	        };
172	
173	        // Plane is always a candidate (cheap; catches misread flats).
174	        let plane = grow::<PlanarSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, 1);
175	        let mut cone_area = None;
176	        let mut sphere_area = None;
177	        if a1 >= flat_eps {
178	            // Curved: a developable (cylinder/cone) is always plausible.
179	            let cone = grow::<ConicalSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, MIN_CYLINDER_FACES);
180	            cone_area = cone.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
181	            consider(cone.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Conical(h), a)), 2, &mut best);
182	            // Curved in both directions → also try a sphere.
183	            if a2 >= 0.3 * a1 {
184	                let sphere = grow::<SphericalSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, MIN_SPHERE_FACES);
185	                sphere_area = sphere.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
186	                consider(sphere.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Spherical(h), a)), 1, &mut best);
187	            }
188	        }
189	        let plane_area = plane.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
190	        consider(plane.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Planar(h), a)), 0, &mut best);
191	
192	        if debug && best.is_none() {
193	            eprintln!(
194	                "    compete: seed {seed} jet=({:.3},{:.3}) class {:?} → NO GROWTH (plane {plane_area:?} cone {cone_area:?} sphere {sphere_area:?})",
195	                jf.k1, jf.k2, curvature_class(jf),
196	            );
197	        }
198	
199	        match best {
200	            Some((hyp, area, dof)) => {
201	                if debug {
202	                    let kind = match hyp {
203	                        CompeteHyp::Planar(_) => "plane",
204	                        CompeteHyp::Conical(ref h) => if h.is_cylinder() { "cyl" } else { "cone" },
205	                        CompeteHyp::Spherical(_) => "sphere",
206	                    };
207	                    eprintln!(
208	                        "    compete: seed {seed} → {kind} ({} faces, area {area:.2}, dof {dof})",
209	                        hyp.faces().len()
210	                    );
211	                }
212	                let idx = committed.len();
213	                for &f in hyp.faces() {
214	                    claimed[f] = true;
215	                    claim_lists[f].push(idx);
216	                }
217	                committed.push(hyp);
218	            }
219	            None => {
220	                // The seed grows nothing analytic; drop it from the pool so
221	                // the loop terminates. It becomes a leftover.
222	                claimed[seed] = true;
223	            }
224	        }
225	    }
226	
227	    resolve_claims(mesh, &region.faces, &mut committed, &claim_lists)
228	}
229	
230	/// Resolve multi-claimed faces to their best-fitting claimant (2× margin to
231	/// leave the larger claimant), rebuild each hypothesis's face/vertex lists,
232	/// drop hypotheses that lost all faces, and collect unclaimed leftovers.
233	fn resolve_claims(
234	    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
235	    region_faces: &[usize],
236	    committed: &mut Vec<CompeteHyp>,
237	    claim_lists: &[Vec<usize>],
238	) -> (Vec<CompeteHyp>, Vec<usize>) {
239	    let hyp_area: Vec<f64> = committed
240	        .iter()
241	        .map(|h| h.faces().iter().map(|&fi| face_area(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices)).sum())
242	        .collect();
243	
244	    let mut assigned: Vec<Vec<usize>> = vec![Vec::new(); committed.len()];
245	    let mut leftovers: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
246	
247	    for &fi in region_faces {
248	        let claimants = &claim_lists[fi];
249	        if claimants.is_empty() {
250	            leftovers.push(fi);
251	            continue;
252	        }
253	        if claimants.len() == 1 {
254	            assigned[claimants[0]].push(fi);
255	            continue;
256	        }
257	        // Best (smallest max-vertex-distance) claimant; the larger-area
258	        // claimant wins ties and only loses by a 2× margin (a true junction
259	        // face fits both sides equally — the margin prevents flapping).
260	        let dist = |ci: usize| max_vertex_dist(mesh, &committed[ci], fi);
261	        let mut order: Vec<usize> = claimants.clone();
262	        order.sort_by(|&a, &b| {
263	            hyp_area[b]
264	                .partial_cmp(&hyp_area[a])
265	                .unwrap()
266	                .then_with(|| a.cmp(&b))
267	        });
268	        let incumbent = order[0]; // largest area
269	        let mut winner = incumbent;
270	        let mut winner_d = dist(incumbent);
271	        for &ci in &order[1..] {
272	            let d = dist(ci);
273	            if d * 2.0 < winner_d {
274	                winner = ci;
275	                winner_d = d;
276	            }
277	        }
278	        assigned[winner].push(fi);
279	    }
280	
281	    let mut out = Vec::new();
282	    for (ci, mut hyp) in committed.drain(..).enumerate() {
283	        let faces = std::mem::take(&mut assigned[ci]);
284	        if faces.is_empty() {
285	            continue;
286	        }
287	        let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &faces);
288	        hyp.set_faces(faces, verts);
289	        out.push(hyp);
290	    }
291	    (out, leftovers)
292	}
293	
294	/// Grow a hypothesis of type \`S\` from \`seed\`: condition an initial fit on a
295	/// jet-similar seed patch, then flood-accept every region face the fit
296	/// validates and refit on the grown support, to a fixpoint. Returns the
297	/// hypothesis, its validated area, and its face list.
298	fn grow<S: SurfaceType>(
299	    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
300	    in_region: &[bool],
301	    seed: usize,
302	    jet: &[JetField],
303	    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
304	    config: &Config,
305	    min_faces: usize,
306	) -> Option<(S::Hypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
307	    let vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm * GROW_VTOL_FACTOR;
308	    let stol = config.surface_tolerance_mm;
309	
310	    let mut accepted = seed_patch(mesh, in_region, seed, jet);
311	    let mut prev = 0usize;
312	    for round in 0..MAX_GROW_ROUNDS {
313	        let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
314	        let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &accepted, &verts, config.verbosity)?;
315	        S::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut fit);
316	        let comp = flood::<S>(mesh, in_region, seed, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol);
317	        if comp.is_empty() {
318	            return None; // the seed does not lie on its own fit — reject
319	        }
320	        if comp.len() == accepted.len() && round > 0 {
321	            accepted = comp;
322	            break;
323	        }
324	        if comp.len() == prev {
325	            // Oscillating between two sizes — stop at the larger.
326	            if comp.len() >= accepted.len() {
327	                accepted = comp;
328	            }
329	            break;
330	        }
331	        prev = accepted.len();
332	        accepted = comp;
333	    }
334	
335	    // Final fit + validated flood.
336	    let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
337	    let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &accepted, &verts, config.verbosity)?;
338	    S::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut fit);
339	    let faces = flood::<S>(mesh, in_region, seed, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol);
340	    if faces.len() < min_faces {
341	        return None;
342	    }
343	    let area: f64 = faces.iter().map(|&fi| face_area(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices)).sum();
344	    let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &faces);
345	    let hyp = S::make_hypothesis(mesh, faces.clone(), verts, &fit);
346	    Some((hyp, area, faces))
347	}
348	
349	/// Connected region-faces reachable from \`seed\` that all validate \`fit\`
350	/// (vertex tolerance + centroid tolerance + orientation coherence).
351	fn flood<S: SurfaceType>(
352	    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
353	    in_region: &[bool],
354	    seed: usize,
355	    fit: &S::Fit,
356	    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
357	    vtol: f64,
358	    stol: f64,
359	) -> Vec<usize> {
360	    if !face_validates::<S>(mesh, seed, fit, centroids, vtol, stol) {
361	        return Vec::new();
362	    }
363	    let mut seen: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
364	    seen.insert(seed);
365	    let mut out = Vec::new();
366	    let mut stack = vec![seed];
367	    while let Some(fi) = stack.pop() {
368	        out.push(fi);
369	        let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
370	        for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
371	            if ni < 0 {
372	                continue;
373	            }
374	            let ni = ni as usize;
375	            if in_region[ni]
376	                && !seen.contains(&ni)
377	                && face_validates::<S>(mesh, ni, fit, centroids, vtol, stol)
378	            {
379	                seen.insert(ni);
380	                stack.push(ni);
381	            }
382	        }
383	    }
384	    out.sort_unstable();
385	    out
386	}
387	
388	/// Does face \`fi\` validate \`fit\` (all vertices ≤ vtol, centroid ≤ stol,
389	/// orientation coherent)?
390	fn face_validates<S: SurfaceType>(
391	    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
392	    fi: usize,
393	    fit: &S::Fit,
394	    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
395	    vtol: f64,
396	    stol: f64,
397	) -> bool {
398	    if !S::convexity_check(fit, mesh, fi) {
399	        return false;
400	    }
401	    let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
402	    let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
403	    for &vi in &face.vertex_indices[..vc] {
404	        let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
405	        if S::vertex_distance(fit, &[v.x, v.y, v.z]) > vtol {
406	            return false;
407	        }
408	    }
409	    S::centroid_distance(fit, &centroids[fi]) <= stol
410	}
411	
412	/// A small jet-similar patch around \`seed\` (same curvature class, magnitude
413	/// within 2×), capped, for a well-conditioned initial fit.
414	fn seed_patch(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, in_region: &[bool], seed: usize, jet: &[JetField]) -> Vec<usize> {
415	    let seed_class = curvature_class(jet[seed]);
416	    let seed_mag = jet[seed].k1.abs().max(jet[seed].k2.abs());
417	    let mut seen: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
418	    seen.insert(seed);
419	    let mut out = vec![seed];
420	    // Depth-limited BFS (frontier by ring).
421	    let mut frontier = vec![seed];
422	    for _depth in 0..MAX_PATCH_DEPTH {
423	        if out.len() >= SEED_PATCH_CAP {
424	            break;
425	        }
426	        let mut next = Vec::new();
427	        for &fi in &frontier {
428	            let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
429	            for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
430	                if ni < 0 {
431	                    continue;
432	                }
433	                let ni = ni as usize;
434	                if !in_region[ni] || seen.contains(&ni) {
435	                    continue;
436	                }
437	                if curvature_class(jet[ni]) != seed_class {
438	                    continue;
439	                }
440	                let mag = jet[ni].k1.abs().max(jet[ni].k2.abs());
441	                if seed_mag > 1e-9 && (mag > 1.5 * seed_mag || mag < 0.667 * seed_mag) {
442	                    continue;
443	                }
444	                seen.insert(ni);
445	                out.push(ni);
446	                next.push(ni);
447	                if out.len() >= SEED_PATCH_CAP {
448	                    break;
449	                }
450	            }
451	        }
452	        frontier = next;
453	    }
454	    out.sort_unstable();
455	    out
456	}
457	
458	#[derive(PartialEq, Eq, Clone, Copy, Debug)]
459	enum CurvClass {
460	    Flat,
461	    Developable,
462	    Spherelike,
463	}
464	
465	fn curvature_class(jf: JetField) -> CurvClass {
466	    let a1 = jf.k1.abs();
467	    let a2 = jf.k2.abs();
468	    // Absolute floor is generous; only used to cluster the seed patch.
469	    if a1 < 1e-4 {
470	        CurvClass::Flat
471	    } else if a2 >= 0.4 * a1 {
472	        CurvClass::Spherelike
473	    } else {
474	        CurvClass::Developable
475	    }
476	}
477	
478	/// Curvature mismatch of a face vs its region neighbors (low = interior).
479	fn neighbor_jet_mismatch(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, fi: usize, jet: &[JetField], in_region: &[bool]) -> f64 {
480	    let jf = jet[fi];
481	    let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
482	    let mut worst = 0.0_f64;
483	    for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
484	        if ni < 0 {
485	            continue;
486	        }
487	        let ni = ni as usize;
488	        if !in_region[ni] {
489	            continue;
490	        }
491	        let jn = jet[ni];
492	        let d = (jf.k1 - jn.k1).abs() + (jf.k2 - jn.k2).abs();
493	        worst = worst.max(d);
494	    }
495	    worst
496	}
497	
498	/// Max distance from face \`fi\`'s vertices to a committed hypothesis.
499	fn max_vertex_dist(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, hyp: &CompeteHyp, fi: usize) -> f64 {
500	    let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
501	    let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
502	    face.vertex_indices[..vc]
503	        .iter()
504	        .map(|&vi| {
505	            let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
506	            let p = [v.x, v.y, v.z];
507	            match hyp {
508	                CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => {
509	                    (h.normal[0] * p[0] + h.normal[1] * p[1] + h.normal[2] * p[2] - h.distance).abs()
510	                }
511	                CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => {
512	                    if h.is_cylinder() {
513	                        super::cylindrical::vertex_to_cylinder_distance(
514	                            v, &h.apex, &h.axis_direction, h.radius_at_apex,
515	                        )
516	                        .abs()
517	                    } else {
518	                        super::conical::vertex_to_cone_distance(v, &h.apex, &h.axis_direction, h.half_angle)
519	                            .abs()
520	                    }
521	                }
522	                CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => {
523	                    super::spherical::vertex_to_sphere_distance(v, &h.center, h.radius).abs()
524	                }
525	            }
526	        })
527	        .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max)
528	}
529	
530	/// Sorted, deduplicated vertex indices of a face set.
531	fn vertices_of(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, faces: &[usize]) -> Vec<usize> {
532	    let mut v: Vec<usize> = faces
533	        .iter()
534	        .flat_map(|&fi| {
535	            let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
536	            mesh.faces[fi].vertex_indices[..vc].iter().copied()
537	        })
538	        .collect();
539	    v.sort_unstable();
540	    v.dedup();
541	    v
542	}
543	
544	/// Largest bounding-box dimension of a region's vertices.
545	fn region_extent(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, region: &Region) -> f64 {
546	    let mut min = [f64::INFINITY; 3];
547	    let mut max = [f64::NEG_INFINITY; 3];
548	    for &vi in &region.vertices {
549	        let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
550	        for (k, c) in [v.x, v.y, v.z].into_iter().enumerate() {
551	            min[k] = min[k].min(c);
552	            max[k] = max[k].max(c);
553	        }
554	    }
555	    (max[0] - min[0]).max(max[1] - min[1]).max(max[2] - min[2])
556	}
557	
```

586	fn fit_torus(
587	    face_indices: &[usize],
588	    vertex_set: &[usize],
589	    faces: &[MeshFace],
590	    vertices: &[MeshVertex],
591	) -> Option<([f64; 3], [f64; 3], f64, f64)> {
592	    let max_err = |fit: &([f64; 3], [f64; 3], f64, f64)| -> f64 {
593	        vertex_set.iter().map(|&vi| {
594	            vertex_to_torus_distance(&vertices[vi], &fit.0, &fit.1, fit.2, fit.3).abs()
595	        }).fold(0.0_f64, f64::max)
596	    };
597	    // Structural validity must be checked BEFORE picking the lowest-error
598	    // candidate: on small corner patches the tube-center method can return
599	    // a self-intersecting fit (minor > major) with a locally lower vertex
600	    // error, which would shadow a valid rescue fit and then be rejected
601	    // downstream.
602	    let valid = |fit: &([f64; 3], [f64; 3], f64, f64)| -> bool {
603	        fit.2 > 0.0 && fit.3 > 0.0 && fit.3 <= fit.2
604	    };
605	
606	    // Try the tube-center and SOR methods and keep the better fit.
607	    let inner = fit_torus_inner(face_indices, vertex_set, faces, vertices);
608	    let sor = fit_torus_sor(face_indices, vertex_set, faces, vertices);
609	    let mut best: Option<(f64, TorusFitTuple)> = None;
610	    for cand in [inner, sor].into_iter().flatten() {
611	        if !valid(&cand) {
612	            continue;
613	        }
614	        let err = max_err(&cand);
615	        if best.as_ref().is_none_or(|(e, _)| err < *e) {
616	            best = Some((err, cand));
617	        }
618	    }
619	
620	    // Multi-axis rescue for small patches, only when the geometric methods
621	    // fail to reach CAD-mesh precision (bounds the extra LM cost to the
622	    // rare hard cases).
623	    const RESCUE_ERR_THRESHOLD: f64 = 1e-4; // mm
624	    if best.as_ref().is_none_or(|(e, _)| *e > RESCUE_ERR_THRESHOLD) {
625	        if let Some(cand) = fit_torus_axes_rescue(face_indices, vertex_set, faces, vertices) {
626	            if valid(&cand) {
627	                let err = max_err(&cand);
628	                if best.as_ref().is_none_or(|(e, _)| err < *e) {
629	                    best = Some((err, cand));
630	                }
631	            }
632	        }
633	    }
634	
635	    best.map(|(_, c)| c)
636	}
637	
638	fn fit_torus_inner(
639	    face_indices: &[usize],
640	    vertex_set: &[usize],
641	    faces: &[MeshFace],
642	    vertices: &[MeshVertex],
643	) -> Option<([f64; 3], [f64; 3], f64, f64)> {
644	    if vertex_set.len() < 7 || face_indices.len() < 3 {
645	        return None;
646	    }
647	
648	    // Sort vertices for deterministic iteration order (avoids floating-point
649	    // accumulation-order differences across runs due to HashSet randomization).
650	    let sorted_verts: Vec<usize> = {
651	        let mut v: Vec<usize> = vertex_set.to_vec();
652	        v.sort_unstable();
653	        v
654	    };
655	
656	    // Step 1: Estimate minor radius from normal-line intersections of adjacent face pairs.
657	    let mut r_estimates: Vec<f64> = Vec::new();
658	    let face_set: HashSet<usize> = face_indices.iter().copied().collect();
659	
660	    for &fi in face_indices {
661	        let n1 = match faces[fi].normal {
662	            Some(n) => n,
663	            None => continue,
664	        };
665	        let c1 = face_centroid(&faces[fi], vertices);
666	        let vc = faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
667	
668	        for &ni in &faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
669	            if ni < 0 { continue; }
670	            let ni = ni as usize;
671	            if !face_set.contains(&ni) { continue; }
672	            if ni <= fi { continue; } // avoid duplicates
673	
674	            let n2 = match faces[ni].normal {
675	                Some(n) => n,
676	                None => continue,
677	            };
678	            let c2 = face_centroid(&faces[ni], vertices);
679	
680	            // Normal-line intersection
681	            if let Some((t, s, dist)) = normal_line_intersection(&c1, &n1, &c2, &n2) {
682	                // For a torus, both t and s should have the same sign (both pointing
683	                // toward the tube center) and similar magnitude ≈ minor_radius.
684	                // The closest-approach distance should be small relative to |t|.
685	                let t_abs = t.abs();
686	                let s_abs = s.abs();
687	                if t_abs < 1e-10 || s_abs < 1e-10 { continue; }
688	
689	                // t and s should have the same sign for consistent curvature
690	                if t * s < 0.0 { continue; }
691	
692	                // The ratio |t|/|s| should be close to 1 for a torus
693	                let ratio = t_abs / s_abs;
694	                if !(0.3..=3.0).contains(&ratio) { continue; }
695	
696	                // The closest-approach distance should be small relative to the radius
697	                let avg_r = (t_abs + s_abs) / 2.0;
698	                if dist > avg_r * 0.5 { continue; }
699	
700	                r_estimates.push(avg_r);
701	            }
702	        }
703	    }
704	
705	    if r_estimates.is_empty() {
706	        return None;
707	    }
708	
709	    // Robust minor radius: 25th percentile of estimates.
710	    // On a torus, adjacent-face normal-line intersections measure a mix of:
711	    //  - tube curvature (≈ r, the minor radius) — from edges along the tube
712	    //  - azimuthal curvature (≈ R ± r) — from edges along the major circle
713	    // The tube-curvature estimates are the SMALLEST values, so we use a low
714	    // percentile instead of median to avoid contamination from azimuthal estimates.
715	    r_estimates.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap());
716	    let minor_r = r_estimates[r_estimates.len() / 4];
717	
718	    if minor_r < 1e-10 {
719	        return None;
720	    }
721	
722	    // Step 2: Determine tube center sign from face normals.
723	    // For convex torus (outer surface), normals point outward from tube center,
724	    // so tube_center = p - r*n. For concave (inner surface), normals point
725	    // toward tube center, so tube_center = p + r*n.
726	    // We try both signs, and use the one that produces a better circle fit.
727	    let best = [1.0_f64, -1.0].iter().filter_map(|&sign| {
728	        // Compute tube centers
729	        let tube_centers: Vec<[f64; 3]> = sorted_verts.iter().filter_map(|&vi| {
730	            // Average normal from all incident seed faces for a better
731	            // surface-normal estimate at the vertex.
732	            let mut avg_n = [0.0_f64; 3];
733	            let mut count = 0u32;
734	            for &fi in face_indices {
735	                let vc = faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
736	                if !faces[fi].vertex_indices[..vc].contains(&vi) { continue; }
737	                if let Some(n) = faces[fi].normal {
738	                    avg_n[0] += n[0];
739	                    avg_n[1] += n[1];
740	                    avg_n[2] += n[2];
741	                    count += 1;
742	                }
743	            }
744	            if count == 0 { return None; }
745	            let len = normalize3(&mut avg_n);
746	            if len < 1e-15 { return None; }
747	            let v = &vertices[vi];
748	            Some([
749	                v.x + sign * minor_r * avg_n[0],
750	                v.y + sign * minor_r * avg_n[1],
751	                v.z + sign * minor_r * avg_n[2],
752	            ])
753	        }).collect();
754	
755	        if tube_centers.len() < 7 { return None; }
756	
757	        // Step 3: Fit 3D circle to tube centers
758	        let (center, axis, major_r) = fit_circle_3d(&tube_centers)?;
759	        if major_r < minor_r * 0.1 {
760	            return None; // Degenerate: major radius too small
761	        }
762	
763	        // Compute RMS residual of tube centers from the fitted circle
764	        let rms = {
765	            let mut sum_sq = 0.0_f64;
766	            for tc in &tube_centers {
767	                let d = [tc[0] - center[0], tc[1] - center[1], tc[2] - center[2]];
768	                let axial = dot3(&d, &axis);
769	                let radial_vec = [d[0] - axial * axis[0], d[1] - axial * axis[1], d[2] - axial * axis[2]];
770	                let radial = (radial_vec[0].powi(2) + radial_vec[1].powi(2) + radial_vec[2].powi(2)).sqrt();
771	                let err = ((radial - major_r).powi(2) + axial * axial).sqrt();
772	                sum_sq += err * err;
773	            }
774	            (sum_sq / tube_centers.len() as f64).sqrt()
775	        };
776	
777	        Some((center, axis, major_r, rms, sign))
778	    }).min_by(|a, b| a.3.partial_cmp(&b.3).unwrap());
779	
780	    let best = match best {
781	        Some(b) => b,
782	        None => {
783	            return None;
784	        }
785	    };
786	
787	    let (center, axis, major_r, rms, _sign) = best;
788	
789	    // Quality gate: reject if tube centers don't form a clean circle.
790	    // This catches mixed convex+concave seeds where tube centers scatter
791	    // between two different major circles. Use tight threshold since valid
792	    // torus fits have tube centers very close to the major circle.
793	    if rms > minor_r * 0.1 {
794	        return None;
795	    }
796	
797	    // Step 4: LM refinement
798	    let points: Vec<[f64; 3]> = sorted_verts.iter()
799	        .map(|&vi| [vertices[vi].x, vertices[vi].y, vertices[vi].z])
800	        .collect();
801	
802	    if points.len() >= 7 {
803	        let problem = TorusLMProblem::new(points, axis, center, major_r, minor_r);
804	        let (result, _report) = LevenbergMarquardt::new().with_patience(150).minimize(problem);
805	        let refined_axis = result.axis_dir_from_params(&result.params);
806	        let refined_center = [result.params[2], result.params[3], result.params[4]];
807	        let refined_major_r = result.params[5];
808	        let refined_minor_r = result.params[6];
809	
810	        if refined_major_r > 0.0 && refined_minor_r > 0.0 {
811	            return Some((refined_center, refined_axis, refined_major_r, refined_minor_r));
812	        }
813	    }
814	
815	    Some((center, axis, major_r, minor_r))
816	}

1439	        if let Some(convex) = fit.convex {
1440	            let fi_convex = determine_convexity(
1441	                &mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices,
1442	                &fit.center, &fit.axis_direction, fit.major_radius,
1443	            );
1444	            fi_convex == convex
1445	        } else {
1446	            true
1447	        }
1448	    }
1449	
1450	    fn determine_convexity(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, face_list: &[usize], fit: &mut Self::Fit) {
1451	        if let Some(&first) = face_list.first() {
1452	            fit.convex = Some(determine_convexity(
1453	                &mesh.faces[first], &mesh.vertices,
1454	                &fit.center, &fit.axis_direction, fit.major_radius,
1455	            ));
1456	        }
1457	    }
1458	
1459	    fn acceptance_test(
1460	        mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
1461	        face_list: &[usize],
1462	        fit: &Self::Fit,
1463	        _config: &BfsConfig,
1464	    ) -> bool {
1465	        if face_list.len() < MIN_TORUS_FACES {
1466	            return false;
1467	        }
1468	        // Reject degenerate giant-radius tori: a straight-edge fillet
1469	        // (cylinder) is the R→∞ limit of a torus, and a short segment of one
1470	        // can be fitted within tolerance by a torus with an absurdly distant
1471	        // axis (observed R ≈ 1500–3600 mm on ~100 mm models). A legitimate
1472	        // torus's major radius cannot exceed the mesh extent. Analogous to
1473	        // the cone apex-distance bound.
1474	        let bb_diag = super::bounding_box_diagonal(&mesh.vertices);
1475	        if fit.major_radius > bb_diag {
1476	            return false;
1477	        }
1478	        // Major circle angular coverage (azimuthal)
1479	        if !angular_coverage_valid(
1480	            face_list, &fit.center, &fit.axis_direction,
1481	            &mesh.faces, &mesh.vertices,
1482	        ) {
1483	            return false;
1484	        }
1485	        // Minor circle angular coverage (tube cross-section)
1486	        minor_circle_coverage_valid(
1487	            face_list, &fit.center, &fit.axis_direction, fit.major_radius,
1488	            &mesh.faces, &mesh.vertices,
1489	        )
1490	    }
1491	
1492	    fn viz_overlays(
1493	        fit: &Self::Fit,
1494	        _mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
1495	        _face_list: &[usize],
1496	    ) -> (Vec<viz::CylinderOverlay>, Vec<viz::ConeOverlay>, Vec<viz::SphereOverlay>, Vec<viz::TorusOverlay>) {
1497	        let torus = viz::TorusOverlay {
1498	            center: fit.center,
1499	            direction: fit.axis_direction,
1500	            major_radius: fit.major_radius,
1501	            minor_radius: fit.minor_radius,
1502	            color: [0.2, 0.4, 1.0, 0.3],
1503	        };
1504	        (Vec::new(), Vec::new(), Vec::new(), vec![torus])
1505	    }
1506	
1507	    fn viz_fit_description(fit: &Self::Fit) -> String {
1508	        format!("R={:.4} r={:.4}", fit.major_radius, fit.minor_radius)
1509	    }
1510	
1511	    fn make_hypothesis(
1512	        mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
1513	        faces: Vec<usize>,
1514	        vertices: Vec<usize>,
1515	        fit: &Self::Fit,
1516	    ) -> ToroidalHypothesis {
1517	        let mut error_max = 0.0_f64;
1518	        let mut centroid_error_max = 0.0_f64;
1519	        let mut error_abs_sum = 0.0_f64;
1520	        for &vi in &vertices {
1521	            let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
1522	            let d = vertex_to_torus_distance(
1523	                v, &fit.center, &fit.axis_direction, fit.major_radius, fit.minor_radius,
1524	            ).abs();
1525	            error_max = error_max.max(d);
1526	            error_abs_sum += d;
1527	        }
1528	        for &fi in &faces {
1529	            let c = face_centroid(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices);
1530	            let v = MeshVertex::from_xyz(c[0], c[1], c[2]);
1531	            let d = vertex_to_torus_distance(
1532	                &v, &fit.center, &fit.axis_direction, fit.major_radius, fit.minor_radius,
1533	            ).abs();
1534	            centroid_error_max = centroid_error_max.max(d);
1535	        }
1536	        ToroidalHypothesis {
1537	            center: fit.center,
1538	            axis_direction: fit.axis_direction,
1539	            major_radius: fit.major_radius,
1540	            minor_radius: fit.minor_radius,
1541	            convex: fit.convex.unwrap_or(true),
1542	            faces,
1543	            vertices,
1544	            error_max,
1545	            centroid_error_max,
1546	            error_abs_sum,
1547	        }
1548	    }
1549	}

1	//! Stage 2.3(a): the whole-region DOF ladder.
2	//!
3	//! Try to explain each smooth region (stage 2.2) with ONE surface, ascending
4	//! degrees of freedom; the first fit that validates wins. Simplest-first
5	//! resolves degeneracy (any plane also lies on an infinite-radius cylinder; a
6	//! single facet is exactly a plane) without coverage heuristics.
7	//!
8	//! | Rung | Surface  | DOF |
9	//! |------|----------|-----|
10	//! | 1    | Plane    | 3   |
11	//! | 2    | Sphere   | 4   |
12	//! | 3    | Conical  | 5/6 | (cylinder-or-cone; the fitter prefers cylinder)
13	//! | 4    | Torus    | 7   |
14	//!
15	//! The conical rung fits a cylinder first and only falls to a cone when the
16	//! cylinder does not validate (\`ConicalSurface::fit_surface\`), so it covers
17	//! both the 5-DOF cylinder and 6-DOF cone cases in DOF order.
18	//!
19	//! **Validation decides all geometry** (Design Principle #1): every claimed
20	//! vertex within \`--vertex-tolerance\`, every claimed face centroid within
21	//! \`--surface-tolerance\`, and orientation coherence (every face normal
22	//! agrees in sign with the surface normal at its centroid — the per-type
23	//! \`convexity_check\`, which also fixes the hypothesis \`convex\` flag). The
24	//! fitters apply their own numeric-validity guards (cone half-angle bounds,
25	//! torus \`0 < r ≤ R\`, sphere radius cap) and return \`None\` outside them.
26	//!
27	//! A region the ladder cannot explain with one surface is a tangent chain
28	//! (pill capsule, rounded-cube shell, pipe-elbow tube). Decomposing those is
29	//! stage 2.3(b) scoped competition (not yet implemented). Until then such a
30	//! region **terminally degrades to per-facet planes** — total coverage can
31	//! never fail, and a single triangle is exactly a plane.
32	
33	use crate::config::Config;
34	use crate::stage1::ConnectedMesh;
35	
36	use super::bfs::SurfaceType;
37	use super::compete::{self, CompeteHyp};
38	use super::conical::ConicalSurface;
39	use super::edges::JetField;
40	use super::planar::{fit_plane, PlanarSurface};
41	use super::regions::Region;
42	use super::spherical::SphericalSurface;
43	use super::toroidal::ToroidalSurface;
44	use super::{
45	    face_centroid, ConicalHypothesis, PlanarHypothesis, SelectedSurface, SphericalHypothesis,
46	    ToroidalHypothesis,
47	};
48	
49	use std::collections::HashSet;
50	
51	/// Regions larger than this many faces are fitted on a deterministic
52	/// stride subsample (validated against the whole region, refit-augmented on
53	/// a near-miss). Bounds fit cost on huge regions — which, lacking scoped
54	/// competition, are the tangent chains that fail the ladder anyway.
55	const MAX_FIT_FACES: usize = 3000;
56	
57	/// Bounded refit rounds: after a subsample fit, the worst validation
58	/// offenders are added to the fit set and the surface is refit, so a large
59	/// clean surface still validates end to end.
60	const MAX_REFIT: usize = 3;
61	
62	/// How many worst-offender faces to fold into the fit set per refit round.
63	const REFIT_ADD: usize = 32;
64	
65	/// Give-up threshold for a refit near-miss: if the worst offender exceeds
66	/// this multiple of \`--surface-tolerance\`, the region is decisively not this
67	/// surface and no amount of augmenting will help.
68	const REFIT_GIVE_UP: f64 = 8.0;
69	
70	/// Largest connected leftover blob competition may leave before its result is
71	/// discarded in favour of a clean per-facet polyhedron (see the fallback in
72	/// \`classify_regions\`). A junction sliver is 1–2 faces; a whole undecomposed
73	/// sub-surface is many more.
74	const LEFTOVER_BLOB_MAX: usize = 8;
75	
76	/// The output of stage 2.3(a): committed hypotheses in the typed vectors plus
77	/// one \`SelectedSurface\` per committed surface. Regions partition the mesh,
78	/// so every mesh face lands in exactly one selected surface.
79	pub(super) struct Classification {
80	    pub planar: Vec<PlanarHypothesis>,
81	    pub conical: Vec<ConicalHypothesis>,
82	    pub spherical: Vec<SphericalHypothesis>,
83	    pub toroidal: Vec<ToroidalHypothesis>,
84	    pub selected: Vec<SelectedSurface>,
85	}
86	
87	impl Classification {
88	    fn new() -> Self {
89	        Classification {
90	            planar: Vec::new(),
91	            conical: Vec::new(),
92	            spherical: Vec::new(),
93	            toroidal: Vec::new(),
94	            selected: Vec::new(),
95	        }
96	    }
97	
98	    fn push_planar(&mut self, hyp: PlanarHypothesis) {
99	        self.selected.push(SelectedSurface::Planar(self.planar.len()));
100	        self.planar.push(hyp);
101	    }
102	    fn push_conical(&mut self, hyp: ConicalHypothesis) {
103	        self.selected
104	            .push(SelectedSurface::Conical(self.conical.len()));
105	        self.conical.push(hyp);
106	    }
107	    fn push_spherical(&mut self, hyp: SphericalHypothesis) {
108	        self.selected
109	            .push(SelectedSurface::Spherical(self.spherical.len()));
110	        self.spherical.push(hyp);
111	    }
112	    fn push_toroidal(&mut self, hyp: ToroidalHypothesis) {
113	        self.selected
114	            .push(SelectedSurface::Toroidal(self.toroidal.len()));
115	        self.toroidal.push(hyp);
116	    }
117	    fn push_compete(&mut self, hyp: CompeteHyp) {
118	        match hyp {
119	            CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => self.push_planar(h),
120	            CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => self.push_conical(h),
121	            CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => self.push_spherical(h),
122	        }
123	    }
124	}
125	
126	/// Run the whole-region ladder over every smooth region; regions the ladder
127	/// cannot explain go to scoped competition (2.3(b)), and whatever competition
128	/// leaves unclaimed degrades to per-facet planes.
129	pub(super) fn classify_regions(
130	    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
131	    regions: &[Region],
132	    jet: &[JetField],
133	    config: &Config,
134	) -> Classification {
135	    let centroids: Vec<[f64; 3]> = mesh
136	        .faces
137	        .iter()
138	        .map(|f| face_centroid(f, &mesh.vertices))
139	        .collect();
140	
141	    let mut cls = Classification::new();
142	    let mut ladder_wins = [0usize; 4]; // plane, sphere, conical, torus
143	    let mut competed_regions = 0usize;
144	    let mut competed_surfaces = 0usize;
145	    let mut facet_faces = 0usize;
146	    for region in regions {
147	        if classify_one_region(mesh, region, &centroids, config, &mut cls, &mut ladder_wins) {
148	            continue;
149	        }
150	        // The ladder cannot explain this region — scoped competition.
151	        competed_regions += 1;
152	        let (hyps, leftovers) = compete::compete_region(mesh, region, jet, &centroids, config);
153	
154	        // A whole undecomposed sub-surface left as per-facet planes (e.g. a
155	        // coarse fillet competition cannot fit) reconstructs to invalid wires
156	        // where it abuts a committed analytic surface — worse than a clean
157	        // polyhedron. When the largest leftover blob is more than a junction
158	        // sliver, discard competition and per-facet the whole region; a clean
159	        // polyhedron is the safe fallback until freeform fitting (2.3(e))
160	        // gives those blobs a principled home. (Healthy competition leaves
161	        // only tiny scattered slivers at junctions.)
162	        if largest_component(mesh, &leftovers) > LEFTOVER_BLOB_MAX {
163	            for &fi in &region.faces {
164	                cls.push_planar(facet_plane(mesh, fi));
165	            }
166	            facet_faces += region.faces.len();
167	            continue;
168	        }
169	
170	        competed_surfaces += hyps.len();
171	        for hyp in hyps {
172	            cls.push_compete(hyp);
173	        }
174	        // Whatever competition left unclaimed → per-facet planes (total
175	        // coverage can never fail; a single triangle is exactly a plane).
176	        facet_faces += leftovers.len();
177	        for fi in leftovers {
178	            cls.push_planar(facet_plane(mesh, fi));
179	        }
180	    }
181	
182	    if config.verbosity >= 1 {
183	        eprintln!(
184	            "  ladder: {} plane, {} sphere, {} cyl/cone, {} torus; competition: {} region(s) → {} surfaces + {} per-facet",
185	            ladder_wins[0], ladder_wins[1], ladder_wins[2], ladder_wins[3],
186	            competed_regions, competed_surfaces, facet_faces,
187	        );
188	    }
189	    cls
190	}
191	
192	/// Try the DOF ladder on one region; commit the first surface that validates.
193	/// Returns true on a committed surface, false if the region fell through
194	/// (caller applies the per-facet fallback).
195	fn classify_one_region(
196	    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
197	    region: &Region,
198	    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
199	    config: &Config,
200	    cls: &mut Classification,
201	    wins: &mut [usize; 4],
202	) -> bool {
203	    let faces = &region.faces;
204	    let verts = &region.vertices;
205	
206	    if let Some(hyp) = fit_validate::<PlanarSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config) {
207	        cls.push_planar(hyp);
208	        wins[0] += 1;
209	        return true;
210	    }
211	    if let Some(hyp) = fit_validate::<SphericalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config) {
212	        cls.push_spherical(hyp);
213	        wins[1] += 1;
214	        return true;
215	    }
216	    if let Some(hyp) = fit_validate::<ConicalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config) {
217	        cls.push_conical(hyp);
218	        wins[2] += 1;
219	        return true;
220	    }
221	    if let Some(hyp) = fit_validate::<ToroidalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config) {
222	        cls.push_toroidal(hyp);
223	        wins[3] += 1;
224	        return true;
225	    }
226	    false
227	}
228	
229	/// Fit surface type \`S\` to a region and validate it against the whole region.
230	/// Large regions fit on a stride subsample, refit-augmented with the worst
231	/// validation offenders on a near-miss. Returns the committed hypothesis, or
232	/// \`None\` if \`S\` cannot explain the region within tolerance.
233	///
234	/// Also the stage-2.4 merge revalidation entry point (\`repair.rs\`): refit a
235	/// candidate union and confirm it validates end to end before committing.
236	pub(super) fn fit_validate<S: SurfaceType>(
237	    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
238	    region_faces: &[usize],
239	    region_verts: &[usize],
240	    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
241	    config: &Config,
242	) -> Option<S::Hypothesis> {
243	    let vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm;
244	    let stol = config.surface_tolerance_mm;
245	
246	    // Initial fit set: the whole region, or a deterministic stride subsample
247	    // when the region is large.
248	    let mut fit_faces: Vec<usize> = if region_faces.len() > MAX_FIT_FACES {
249	        let stride = region_faces.len().div_ceil(MAX_FIT_FACES);
250	        region_faces.iter().copied().step_by(stride).collect()
251	    } else {
252	        region_faces.to_vec()
253	    };
254	
255	    for _round in 0..=MAX_REFIT {
256	        let fit_verts = vertices_of(mesh, &fit_faces);
257	        let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &fit_faces, &fit_verts, config.verbosity)?;
258	        S::determine_convexity(mesh, region_faces, &mut fit);
259	
260	        // Validate every region face; collect near-miss offenders for a refit.
261	        let mut offenders: Vec<(f64, usize)> = Vec::new();
262	        for &fi in region_faces {
263	            // Orientation coherence: a face whose normal disagrees with the
264	            // fitted surface's convexity means the region is not one clean
265	            // surface of type S — no refit rescues that.
266	            if !S::convexity_check(&fit, mesh, fi) {
267	                return None;
268	            }
269	            let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
270	            let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
271	            let mut excess = 0.0_f64;
272	            for &vi in &face.vertex_indices[..vc] {
273	                let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
274	                let d = S::vertex_distance(&fit, &[v.x, v.y, v.z]);
275	                if d > vtol {
276	                    excess = excess.max(d - vtol);
277	                }
278	            }
279	            let cd = S::centroid_distance(&fit, &centroids[fi]);
280	            if cd > stol {
281	                excess = excess.max(cd - stol);
282	            }
283	            if excess > 0.0 {
284	                offenders.push((excess, fi));
285	            }
286	        }
287	
288	        if offenders.is_empty() {
289	            return Some(S::make_hypothesis(
290	                mesh,
291	                region_faces.to_vec(),
292	                region_verts.to_vec(),
293	                &fit,
294	            ));
295	        }
296	
297	        // Near-miss handling: if the worst offender is far out, the region is
298	        // decisively not this surface. Otherwise fold the worst offenders
299	        // into the fit set and refit (bounded rounds).
300	        offenders.sort_by(|a, b| {
301	            b.0.partial_cmp(&a.0)
302	                .unwrap()
303	                .then_with(|| a.1.cmp(&b.1))
304	        });
305	        if offenders[0].0 > REFIT_GIVE_UP * stol {
306	            return None;
307	        }
308	        let before = fit_faces.len();
309	        let existing: HashSet<usize> = fit_faces.iter().copied().collect();
310	        for &(_, fi) in offenders.iter().take(REFIT_ADD) {
311	            if !existing.contains(&fi) {
312	                fit_faces.push(fi);
313	            }
314	        }
315	        fit_faces.sort_unstable();
316	        fit_faces.dedup();
317	        if fit_faces.len() == before {
318	            // No new faces to learn from — cannot improve.
319	            return None;
320	        }
321	    }
322	    None
323	}
324	
325	/// Size of the largest mesh-connected component within a face set.
326	fn largest_component(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, faces: &[usize]) -> usize {
327	    let member: HashSet<usize> = faces.iter().copied().collect();
328	    let mut seen: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
329	    let mut largest = 0usize;
330	    for &start in faces {
331	        if seen.contains(&start) {
332	            continue;
333	        }
334	        let mut size = 0usize;
335	        let mut stack = vec![start];
336	        seen.insert(start);
337	        while let Some(fi) = stack.pop() {
338	            size += 1;
339	            let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
340	            for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
341	                if ni >= 0 {
342	                    let ni = ni as usize;
343	                    if member.contains(&ni) && seen.insert(ni) {
344	                        stack.push(ni);
345	                    }
346	                }
347	            }
348	        }
349	        largest = largest.max(size);
350	    }
351	    largest
352	}
353	
354	/// Sorted, deduplicated vertex indices of a face set.
355	fn vertices_of(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, faces: &[usize]) -> Vec<usize> {
356	    let mut v: Vec<usize> = faces
357	        .iter()
358	        .flat_map(|&fi| {
359	            let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
360	            mesh.faces[fi].vertex_indices[..vc].iter().copied()
361	        })
362	        .collect();
363	    v.sort_unstable();
364	    v.dedup();
365	    v
366	}
367	
368	/// A single-face planar hypothesis (the terminal per-facet fallback).
369	fn facet_plane(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, fi: usize) -> PlanarHypothesis {
370	    let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
371	    let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
372	    let vertices: Vec<usize> = face.vertex_indices[..vc].to_vec();
373	    let vertex_set: HashSet<usize> = vertices.iter().copied().collect();
374	    let (normal, distance) = fit_plane(&[fi], &vertex_set, &mesh.faces, &mesh.vertices);
375	    let mut error_max = f64::NEG_INFINITY;
376	    let mut error_min = f64::INFINITY;
377	    let mut error_abs_sum = 0.0_f64;
378	    for &vi in &vertices {
379	        let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
380	        let d = normal[0] * v.x + normal[1] * v.y + normal[2] * v.z - distance;

> With the tolerance set to 1.5e-3, an osculating torus along a latitude circle shows deviation growing with band width. The old BFS approach needed the large_sphere mask to avoid bogus fits dying during selection. In the competition scenario, a sphere seed grows across a whole corner (roughly 8 faces), while a torus band could grow across the band plus adjacent fillet rows — raising the question of whether the torus flood could exceed the sphere's area and get committed. From a corner sphere seed, an osculating torus along a latitude ring might also validate adjacent fillet cylinder faces if they're tangent, though the fillet cylinder axis is straight while the torus curves.
> 
> Now I'm working through the geometry: the torus deviates from the sphere by roughly w⁴/(8r³) over a band width w, and for a rounded_cube_fine with a few quad rows per octant, I need to check if a torus band plus one fillet row could exceed the octant area. An octant area is about 6.3mm², and a full latitude band would need width greater than r/2 (1mm) to beat the sphere — but at 1.5e-3 tolerance, the band width validating the torus is much narrower than that threshold.
> 
> The torus loses on area grounds, and the sphere also wins on degrees of freedom. I should add a safety gate to prevent spurious tori from appearing on spheres — something like skipping the torus candidate when the sphere validates better, or checking that the principal curvature ratio doesn't look sphere-like.
> 
> Adding a gate on the curvature ratio (a2 ≤ 0.8·a1) is cheap and avoids the sphere-band risk while saving Levenberg-Marquardt cost on sphere seeds. For inner-bend saddle seeds, the curvature ratio depends on the torus geometry — tight bends can exceed the threshold, but outer-bend seeds still cover the region. Fillets stay well within bounds.

I have the full picture now. Before writing code, here's my design assessment:

**Design reconsideration (per your prompt):** The kickoff's "detect stall → promote" heuristic has a tuning problem it itself flags ("when has a cylinder stalled vs. finished?"). I'm restructuring around two complementary, more general mechanisms:

1. **Torus as a first-class competition candidate** — any curved seed also grows a `ToroidalSurface` candidate (jet-pruned: skipped when the seed reads sphere-like, κ2 ≈ κ1). This recognizes tori *generally* — fillet tori, concave fillets, bend tubes — with no stall heuristic: once cylinders commit, unclaimed bend/fillet faces become seeds themselves, and largest-validated-area decides. Guard: reject giant-major-radius degenerate fits (R > bbox diagonal), mirroring the ladder's acceptance test.

2. **Bend promotion as the user suggested** — after a cylinder candidate grows, examine its frontier. The key insight that makes this *better* than jet-class seeding: the cylinder fit itself locates the tangency (frontier faces failed cylinder validation), so "frontier + off-cylinder neighbors" is a guaranteed pure-bend patch that cannot straddle the junction the way a jet-similar patch can (the step-4 cone↔cylinder straddle problem, which recurs here since bend and straight tube share κ1). The promoted torus is just another candidate at the same seed — no special commit logic.

const GROW_VTOL_FACTOR: f64 = 30.0;

/// A committed analytic hypothesis from competition, tagged by type.
pub(super) enum CompeteHyp {
    Planar(PlanarHypothesis),
    Conical(ConicalHypothesis),
    Spherical(SphericalHypothesis),
}

impl CompeteHyp {
    fn faces(&self) -> &[usize] {
        match self {
            CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => &h.faces,
            CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => &h.faces,
            CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => &h.faces,
        }
    }
    fn set_faces(&mut self, faces: Vec<usize>, verts: Vec<usize>) {
        match self {
            CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => {
                h.faces = faces;
                h.vertices = verts;
            }
            CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => {
                h.faces = faces;
                h.vertices = verts;
            }
            CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => {
                h.faces = faces;
                h.vertices = verts;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

**After:**
```
const GROW_VTOL_FACTOR: f64 = 30.0;

/// Max BFS depth of the pure-bend fit patch grown past a stalled cylinder's
/// frontier (bent-tube promotion). A few rows of bend arc condition the
/// 7-DOF torus fit; the flood then claims the rest.
const BEND_PATCH_DEPTH: usize = 4;

/// Face cap of the pure-bend fit patch.
const BEND_PATCH_CAP: usize = 64;

/// How many flood-start faces a toroidal candidate tries before giving up
/// (each attempt refits from the same initial patch, so extras only matter
/// when the best start happens not to validate its own fit).
const MAX_TORUS_STARTS: usize = 4;

/// A committed analytic hypothesis from competition, tagged by type.
pub(super) enum CompeteHyp {
    Planar(PlanarHypothesis),
    Conical(ConicalHypothesis),
    Spherical(SphericalHypothesis),
    Toroidal(ToroidalHypothesis),
}

impl CompeteHyp {
    fn faces(&self) -> &[usize] {
        match self {
            CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => &h.faces,
            CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => &h.faces,
            CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => &h.faces,
            CompeteHyp::Toroidal(h) => &h.faces,
        }
    }
    fn set_faces(&mut self, faces: Vec<usize>, verts: Vec<usize>) {
        match self {
            CompeteHyp::Planar(h) => {
                h.faces = faces;
                h.vertices = verts;
            }
            CompeteHyp::Conical(h) => {
                h.faces = faces;
                h.vertices = verts;
            }
            CompeteHyp::Spherical(h) => {
                h.faces = faces;
                h.vertices = verts;
            }
            CompeteHyp::Toroidal(h) => {
                h.faces = faces;
                h.vertices = verts;
            }
        }
    }
}

        // Plane is always a candidate (cheap; catches misread flats).
        let plane = grow::<PlanarSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, 1);
        let mut cone_area = None;
        let mut sphere_area = None;
        let mut torus_area = None;
        if a1 >= flat_eps {
            // Curved: a developable (cylinder/cone) is always plausible.
            let cone = grow::<ConicalSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, MIN_CYLINDER_FACES);
            cone_area = cone.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));

            // Toroidal candidate: a direct grow from this seed (fillet tori,
            // bend tubes), skipped when the seed reads sphere-like — every
            // adjacent quad-row band of a sphere lies near some torus, so a
            // (κ,κ) seed's torus is at best a bogus band that loses to the
            // sphere anyway. A torus seed reads |κ2| < |κ1| (κ2 varies
            // azimuthally around the tube; it only approaches κ1 near the
            // degenerate R≈r hole of a donut — a whole-region ladder case,
            // not a competition case).
            let mut torus = if a2 < 0.8 * a1 {
                let patch = seed_patch(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet);
                torus_candidate(mesh, &in_region, &patch, &[seed], centroids, config)
            } else {
                None
            };
            // Bent-tube promotion: a cylinder that stalled against
            // tube-continuation faces is the developable prefix of a bent
            // tube; grow a torus from the pure-bend patch past the stall.
            if let Some((cyl, _, cyl_faces)) = cone.as_ref() {
                if cyl.is_cylinder() {
                    let promoted =
                        promote_bend(mesh, &in_region, cyl, cyl_faces, jet, centroids, config);
                    if let Some(p) = promoted {
                        if torus.as_ref().is_none_or(|(_, a, _)| p.1 > *a) {
                            torus = Some(p);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            torus_area = torus.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));

            consider(cone.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Conical(h), a)), 2, &mut best);
            // Curved in both directions → also try a sphere.
            if a2 >= 0.3 * a1 {
                let sphere = grow::<SphericalSurface>(mesh, &in_region, seed, jet, centroids, config, MIN_SPHERE_FACES);
                sphere_area = sphere.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
                consider(sphere.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Spherical(h), a)), 1, &mut best);
            }
            consider(torus.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Toroidal(h), a)), 3, &mut best);
        }
        let plane_area = plane.as_ref().map(|(_, a, f)| (*a, f.len()));
        consider(plane.map(|(h, a, _)| (CompeteHyp::Planar(h), a)), 0, &mut best);

        if debug && best.is_none() {
            eprintln!(
                "    compete: seed {seed} jet=({:.3},{:.3}) class {:?} → NO GROWTH (plane {plane_area:?} cone {cone_area:?} sphere {sphere_area:?} torus {torus_area:?})",
                jf.k1, jf.k2, curvature_class(jf),
            );
        }

/// Grow a hypothesis of type `S` from `seed`: condition an initial fit on a
/// jet-similar seed patch, then flood-accept every region face the fit
/// validates and refit on the grown support, to a fixpoint. Returns the
/// hypothesis, its validated area, and its face list.
fn grow<S: SurfaceType>(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    in_region: &[bool],
    seed: usize,
    jet: &[JetField],
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
    min_faces: usize,
) -> Option<(S::Hypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
    let vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm * GROW_VTOL_FACTOR;
    let stol = config.surface_tolerance_mm;

    let mut accepted = seed_patch(mesh, in_region, seed, jet);
    let mut prev = 0usize;
    for round in 0..MAX_GROW_ROUNDS {
        let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
        let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &accepted, &verts, config.verbosity)?;
        S::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut fit);
        let comp = flood::<S>(mesh, in_region, seed, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol);
        if comp.is_empty() {
            return None; // the seed does not lie on its own fit — reject
        }
        if comp.len() == accepted.len() && round > 0 {
            accepted = comp;
            break;
        }
        if comp.len() == prev {
            // Oscillating between two sizes — stop at the larger.
            if comp.len() >= accepted.len() {
                accepted = comp;
            }
            break;
        }
        prev = accepted.len();
        accepted = comp;
    }

    // Final fit + validated flood.
    let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
    let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &accepted, &verts, config.verbosity)?;
    S::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut fit);
    let faces = flood::<S>(mesh, in_region, seed, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol);
    if faces.len() < min_faces {
        return None;
    }
    let area: f64 = faces.iter().map(|&fi| face_area(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices)).sum();
    let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &faces);
    let hyp = S::make_hypothesis(mesh, faces.clone(), verts, &fit);
    Some((hyp, area, faces))
}
```

**After:**
```
/// Grow a hypothesis of type `S` from `seed`: condition an initial fit on a
/// jet-similar seed patch, then flood-accept every region face the fit
/// validates and refit on the grown support, to a fixpoint. Returns the
/// hypothesis, its validated area, and its face list.
fn grow<S: SurfaceType>(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    in_region: &[bool],
    seed: usize,
    jet: &[JetField],
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
    min_faces: usize,
) -> Option<(S::Hypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
    let patch = seed_patch(mesh, in_region, seed, jet);
    grow_from::<S>(mesh, in_region, seed, patch, centroids, config, min_faces)
}

/// The grow loop proper, from an explicit initial fit patch and flood start:
/// fit the patch, flood-accept every region face the fit validates, refit on
/// the grown support, to a fixpoint. Returns the hypothesis, its validated
/// area, and its face list.
fn grow_from<S: SurfaceType>(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    in_region: &[bool],
    start: usize,
    initial: Vec<usize>,
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
    min_faces: usize,
) -> Option<(S::Hypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
    let vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm * GROW_VTOL_FACTOR;
    let stol = config.surface_tolerance_mm;

    let mut accepted = initial;
    let mut prev = 0usize;
    for round in 0..MAX_GROW_ROUNDS {
        let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
        let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &accepted, &verts, config.verbosity)?;
        S::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut fit);
        let comp = flood::<S>(mesh, in_region, start, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol);
        if comp.is_empty() {
            return None; // the start face does not lie on its own fit — reject
        }
        if comp.len() == accepted.len() && round > 0 {
            accepted = comp;
            break;
        }
        if comp.len() == prev {
            // Oscillating between two sizes — stop at the larger.
            if comp.len() >= accepted.len() {
                accepted = comp;
            }
            break;
        }
        prev = accepted.len();
        accepted = comp;
    }

    // Final fit + validated flood.
    let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &accepted);
    let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &accepted, &verts, config.verbosity)?;
    S::determine_convexity(mesh, &accepted, &mut fit);
    let faces = flood::<S>(mesh, in_region, start, &fit, centroids, vtol, stol);
    if faces.len() < min_faces {
        return None;
    }
    let area: f64 = faces.iter().map(|&fi| face_area(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices)).sum();
    let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &faces);
    let hyp = S::make_hypothesis(mesh, faces.clone(), verts, &fit);
    Some((hyp, area, faces))
}

/// Grow a toroidal candidate from an initial fit patch, trying flood starts
/// in order until one grows. Rejects degenerate giant-major-radius fits: a
/// short segment of straight cylinder fits a torus with an absurdly distant
/// axis (observed R ≈ 1500–3600 mm on ~100 mm models under the old BFS), and
/// a legitimate torus's major radius cannot exceed the mesh extent — the
/// same guard as the ladder's acceptance test.
fn torus_candidate(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    in_region: &[bool],
    initial: &[usize],
    starts: &[usize],
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
) -> Option<(ToroidalHypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
    let bb_diag = bounding_box_diagonal(&mesh.vertices);
    for &start in starts.iter().take(MAX_TORUS_STARTS) {
        if let Some((hyp, area, faces)) = grow_from::<ToroidalSurface>(
            mesh,
            in_region,
            start,
            initial.to_vec(),
            centroids,
            config,
            MIN_TORUS_FACES,
        ) {
            if hyp.major_radius <= bb_diag {
                return Some((hyp, area, faces));
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

/// Stage 2.3(c) bent-tube promotion. A cylinder flood that stalled against
/// region faces whose jet continues the tube — κ1 ≈ the tube's 1/r, |κ2|
/// well below κ1 (the same tube swinging around a bend, not a cap plane's
/// (0,0) or an end sphere's (κ,κ)) — is the developable prefix of a bent
/// tube. Fit a torus to the pure-bend patch just past the stall line
/// (frontier faces plus their off-cylinder tube-jet neighbours) and grow it;
/// the result competes on validated area like any other candidate.
///
/// The cylinder fit itself locates the tangency — frontier faces failed
/// cylinder validation — so unlike a jet-class seed patch (which cannot tell
/// bend from straight tube: both read the same κ1) this patch cannot
/// straddle the junction.
fn promote_bend(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    in_region: &[bool],
    cyl: &ConicalHypothesis,
    cyl_faces: &[usize],
    jet: &[JetField],
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
) -> Option<(ToroidalHypothesis, f64, Vec<usize>)> {
    if cyl.radius_at_apex <= 0.0 {
        return None;
    }
    let k_tube = 1.0 / cyl.radius_at_apex;
    let grow_vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm * GROW_VTOL_FACTOR;

    // Max vertex distance to the stalled cylinder — the "past the tangency"
    // test (frontier faces failed the flood at exactly this tolerance).
    let off_cyl_dist = |fi: usize| -> f64 {
        let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
        let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
        face.vertex_indices[..vc]
            .iter()
            .map(|&vi| {
                super::cylindrical::vertex_to_cylinder_distance(
                    &mesh.vertices[vi],
                    &cyl.apex,
                    &cyl.axis_direction,
                    cyl.radius_at_apex,
                )
                .abs()
            })
            .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max)
    };
    // Tube-continuation jet signature. The κ2 bound is generous: a bend's
    // inner (saddle) side reads |κ2| = 1/(R−r), which approaches κ1 = 1/r
    // only for very tight bends; junction rows have contaminated jets and
    // are excluded from the fit patch either way (the flood claims them).
    let tube_jet = |fi: usize| -> bool {
        let a1 = jet[fi].k1.abs();
        a1 >= 0.5 * k_tube && a1 <= 2.0 * k_tube && jet[fi].k2.abs() <= 0.8 * a1
    };

    // Frontier: in-region neighbours of the cylinder support that are past
    // the tangency and continue the tube.
    let mut seen: HashSet<usize> = cyl_faces.iter().copied().collect();
    let mut frontier: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
    for &fi in cyl_faces {
        let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
        for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
            if ni < 0 {
                continue;
            }
            let ni = ni as usize;
            if !in_region[ni] || !seen.insert(ni) {
                continue;
            }
            if off_cyl_dist(ni) > grow_vtol && tube_jet(ni) {
                frontier.push(ni);
            }
        }
    }
    if frontier.is_empty() {
        return None; // finished a straight pipe (or stalled at a cap/sphere)
    }
    frontier.sort_unstable();

    // Pure-bend fit patch: depth-limited multi-source BFS from the frontier
    // through off-cylinder tube-jet faces. (Neighbours examined and rejected
    // above stay excluded — junction rows with contaminated jets would only
    // pollute the fit.)
    let mut patch: Vec<usize> = frontier.clone();
    let mut ring = frontier.clone();
    for _depth in 0..BEND_PATCH_DEPTH {
        if patch.len() >= BEND_PATCH_CAP {
            break;
        }
        let mut next: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
        for &fi in &ring {
            let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
            for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
                if ni < 0 {
                    continue;
                }
                let ni = ni as usize;
                if !in_region[ni] || seen.contains(&ni) {
                    continue;
                }
                if off_cyl_dist(ni) <= grow_vtol || !tube_jet(ni) {
                    continue;
                }
                seen.insert(ni);
                patch.push(ni);
                next.push(ni);
                if patch.len() >= BEND_PATCH_CAP {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        if next.is_empty() {
            break;
        }
        ring = next;
    }
    patch.sort_unstable();

    // Flood starts: deepest-off-cylinder first — the faces most surely
    // interior to the bend (frontier faces sit on the junction row and may
    // not validate the torus fit themselves).
    let mut starts = patch.clone();
    starts.sort_by(|&a, &b| {
        off_cyl_dist(b)
            .partial_cmp(&off_cyl_dist(a))
            .unwrap()
            .then_with(|| a.cmp(&b))
    });

    torus_candidate(mesh, in_region, &patch, &starts, centroids, config)
}
```

# Instructions for LLM Coding Assistants

This file contains guidelines for AI/LLM coding assistants working on this project. Read ALL of AGENTS.md - no just the first 100 lines.

The shell is zsh. Use zsh quoting conventions. "echo ===" will fail, and globs in flags must be quoted.

### Commit Checklist (MANDATORY — verify before every commit)

- \`cargo clippy\` emits no warnings.
- Build passes with no errors or warnings.

## Project Layout

This is a Rust project. Key locations:

- \`README.md\` - Program usage. Read it.
- \`DEVELOPMENT_PLAN.md\` - Development plan. Read it.
- \`Cargo.toml\` — Rust project manifest
- \`src/\` — Rust source files
  - \`bin/brepper.rs\` — the main program
  - \`bin/step_measure_tolerance.rs\` — utility to measure distances from STL to correpsonding STEP
  - \`bin/viewer.rs\` — GUI to visualize STL or STEP files
- \`scripts/\` — shell scripts for batch operations over test data
  - \`step_distance_table.sh\` - runs \`step_measure_tolerance\` over all STL/STEP pairs and summarizes statistics
- \`tests/\` — STL and STEP test file pairs
  - \`manual/\` — handcrafted simple models
  - \`onshape/\` — models exported from Onshape
  - \`ccad/generated/\` — models generated via CodeCAD

### OCCT / opencascade-rs

The project uses the \`opencascade-sys\` crate (auto-generated Rust FFI bindings to OpenCASCADE Technology) via a path dependency. MANDATORY: All useful C++ OCCT APIs should be available in the binding - if you want to use one that doesn't seem to be bound, complain loudly about it AND STOP.

\`\`\`
../opencascade-rs/crates/opencascade-sys   ← Rust FFI bindings (8700+ types)
../opencascade-rs/crates/opencascade-sys/PORTING.md  ← Naming conventions & usage patterns
../opencascade-rs/crates/opencascade-sys/generated/  ← All generated .rs modules
../opencascade-rs/crates/occt-sys/OCCT/src/  ← OCCT C++ source (for reading docs/headers)
../opencascade-rs/crates/occt-sys/OCCT/dox/  ← OCCT C++ documentation
\`\`\`

The \`builtin\` feature (default) compiles OCCT from source — first build takes ~8 minutes. Subsequent builds are fast.

## Commit Messages

All commit messages must include:

1. **A clear description** of the changes made
2. **The AI agent/model** used to generate the changes (e.g., "Claude Sonnet 4 (Anthropic, 2025)")
3. **The user prompts** that led to the changes

Format:
\`\`\`
<descriptive commit title>

<detailed description of changes>

---
Generated with assistance from <model name> (<provider>, <year>)
```
</details>

```
Stage 2 rewrite: step 5 (toroidal competition + bent-tube promotion)

Stage 2.3(c): tori now compete in scoped fit competition (compete.rs).
Two mechanisms, both riding the existing 7-DOF torus fitter — the
degenerate-limit k=1/R model and medial-spine seeding were NOT needed
(they remain contingency for strip/coarse meshes):

- Direct toroidal candidate: any curved, non-sphere-like seed
  (|k2| < 0.8|k1|) also grows a ToroidalSurface candidate, so fillet
  tori and bend tubes are recognized generally, not only next to a
  stalled cylinder. Sphere-like seeds are skipped because every
  adjacent quad-row band of a sphere lies near some torus.
- Bent-tube promotion: after a cylinder candidate grows, its frontier
  is scanned for tube-continuation jet (k1 within 2x of the tube's 1/r,
  |k2| <= 0.8 k1 — excludes cap planes (0,0) and end spheres (k,k)).
  If present, a torus is fitted to the pure-bend patch just past the
  stall line (frontier + off-cylinder tube-jet neighbours, depth <= 4,
  cap 64) and grown; flood starts try deepest-off-cylinder faces first.
  The cylinder fit itself locates the tangency, so unlike a jet-class
  seed patch this one cannot straddle the junction (bend and straight
  tube share k1). The promoted torus is just another candidate at the
  same seed — largest validated area still decides.

Degenerate guard: torus candidates with major radius > mesh bbox
diagonal are rejected (a short straight-cylinder segment fits a torus
with an absurdly distant axis), mirroring the ladder acceptance test.

Plumbing: CompeteHyp::Toroidal variant (faces/set_faces/
max_vertex_dist/push_compete arms); grow() split into grow() +
grow_from() taking an explicit initial patch and flood start; DOF
tiebreak torus=3.

Smoke (pipe_elbow_10_medium): 2 planes + 2 cylinders + 1 torus,
1 valid solid, 0 per-facet leftovers — the step-5 acceptance shape on
the first run. stage2_competition tests (rounded_cube 6+12+8, nosecone)
still green. Full corpus verification is deliberately deferred to the
next session per the split-implementation/debug workflow.

---
name: step-5-debug
description: Handoff for the step-5 DEBUG session — toroidal competition + bent-tube promotion landed d7cdb6a; what to test and the likely failure points
metadata:
